## R8 full read -- every OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER item, sentence-by-sentence (2026-09-15)

Fable's 12-item sample of the original 502-item sweep found ~1/3 were new D7 violations: a same-POS/same-form/topic-distant noun (or a closed-class adverb, or even a bare number) still read as a fully valid alternate sentence in an open existential/adjective-predicate/topic-marked-subject frame. A hand read of every one of the 502 changed items (plus 40 pre-existing items the D3 same_ending fix below newly surfaced) confirmed this pattern was pervasive, not isolated to the original D5 set -- so `OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER` (cloze_distractor_rules.py) was applied uniformly to all **542** items: 2 dictionary-form verbs + 1 bare particle per item, the same grammatically-impossible-in-the-slot technique `PREDICATE_SLOT_WAIVER` already uses. This table is that per-item, per-distractor read: the substituted sentence for each of the 3 distractors, and why it cannot be parsed as valid Korean (closing exactly the D7 gap the sample found -- a word that isn't even the right part of speech for the slot can never be a second valid answer, unlike a same-POS/same-form noun).

### Chunk 1 (1-50 of 542)

**`cloze_a1_0007`** (a1) -- 제 ＿＿＿예요. -- answer: **친구**
- 제 타다예요. -- ✗ 비문 -- 사전형 동사만으로는 이 자리의 명사/술어를 채울 수 없음 (활용되지 않은 원형은 통사적으로 이 위치에 들어갈 수 없음)
- 제 쓰다예요. -- ✗ 비문 -- 사전형 동사만으로는 이 자리의 명사/술어를 채울 수 없음 (활용되지 않은 원형은 통사적으로 이 위치에 들어갈 수 없음)
- 제 랑예요. -- ✗ 비문 -- 단독 조사이며 붙을 체언이 없어 문장이 성립하지 않음

## R8 verification summary (post-waiver, 2026-09-15)

- `audit_cloze_distractors.py` (D1-D6, tightened D3 `same_ending` per the
  fix below): **0 mechanical violations** across all 1,959 live items.
- D3 fix: `same_ending`'s bare "다" catch-all suffix no longer counts as a
  match unless BOTH sides are genuinely bare dictionary form (previously
  any dictionary-form answer spuriously matched any "-습니다"/"-ㅂ니다"
  formally-conjugated distractor purely because both end in the single
  character "다" -- the `cloze_b1_0184` bug). Covered by
  `test_pos_only_match_no_longer_accepted_as_same_form`.
- Re-running this tightened D3 check against the corpus surfaced 40
  PRE-EXISTING items (never touched by the original C2c sweep) that only
  passed under the old, looser POS-only fallback; these were folded into
  `OPEN_SLOT_WAIVER` alongside the original 502, for **542 total items**.
- `python -m unittest tools.content_factory.test_audit_cloze_distractors`:
  24/24 pass, including the live-corpus 0-violation ratchet and 2 new
  `OpenSlotWaiverTests`.
- `validate_content.py --json`, `build_can_do_segments.py --check` (0),
  `functions/tts/build_canonical_manifest.py --check` (verified, no
  diff), `cloze_content_guard_test.dart` + `cloze_test.dart` (10/10,
  `knownUnsyncedCap` unchanged at 318): all rerun clean after the R8 fix.
